<div class="et_pb_heading_24 et_pb_heading et_pb_module et_block_module preset--module--divi-heading--f0c675ea-2574-4d0e-b725-30f8550a8550"><div class="et_pb_heading_container"><h4 class="et_pb_module_header">Discover Charleston’s Finest Properties</h4></div></div>

<div class="et_pb_heading_25 et_pb_heading et_pb_module et_block_module preset--module--divi-heading--ddeb8c09-9078-4424-bc15-2efb6572e28e"><div class="et_pb_heading_container"><h1 class="et_pb_module_header">Michael Teibert with Carolina One Real Estate</h1></div></div>

<div class="et_pb_text_36 et_pb_text et_pb_bg_layout_light et_pb_module et_block_module preset--module--divi-text--0504de1e-f5ff-4281-ba60-cbbe4edf98bc"><div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>Experience the charm of Charleston with our dedicated team, committed to finding your perfect home.</p>

<div class="et_pb_heading_26 et_pb_heading et_pb_module et_block_module preset--module--divi-heading--default"><div class="et_pb_heading_container"><h2 class="et_pb_module_header">My Southern Roots</h2></div></div>

<div class="et_pb_text_37 et_pb_text et_pb_bg_layout_light et_pb_module et_block_module preset--module--divi-text--default"><div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>At Teibert &amp; Associates Real Estate, we pride ourselves on our deep-rooted commitment to southern charm and hospitality. Our team embodies the warmth and friendliness that Charleston is known for, ensuring every client feels valued and understood. With a focus on trust and integrity, we strive to make every real estate transaction as smooth and pleasant as a southern breeze. Our mission is to connect people with their dream homes while fostering lasting relationships built on respect and care.</p>
</div></div>
